Task: write the WGSL module for each pass as a NEW standalone file under `engine/render/gpu/wgsl/` (line-by-line port of the GLSL in `engine/render/gpu/glsl/`, constants interpolated from the same JS imports, no raw `%`, uniform block via `uniformBlock.js`), register it in `wgsl/index.js` (WGSL_MODULES), extend `wgsl.test.js` string rules, and pass `node tools/capture-browser.mjs --mode wgsl --backend webgpu --port 96xx` (0 compile errors). B2 2026-10-09 (ALPHA-01f c shadow) -> arch-review: (1) JS twin needed NO production code change - `rasterJS.js`'s shared `rasterRange`/`rasterFanTri` (used identically by the colour target and the depth-only/shadow target) already masks per-range for `DRAW_INSTANCED` items as of step (a), since `target.depthOnly` only changes which branch of `rasterFanTri` runs, not whether `info.maskW` is set. New oracle `engine/mesh/rasterInstancedMaskShadow.test.js` (built via `DrawList.addInstances` directly, no `DRAW_FLAG_ONE_PART` - same isolation as step a) proves it: N instanced depth-only casters == N static masked depth-only casters, byte-identical zbuf; instanced shadow-twin zbuf == instanced colour-pass zbuf (ALPHA-01b's "shadow twin skips the same fragments" rule, now through the instanced path); mutation guard (masked-range shadow coverage 30-70% of the fully-opaque reference; confirmed in a scratch probe that nulling `ctx.maskAtlas` - the "mask ignored" failure mode - pushes coverage far outside that band); opaque-only instanced casters (no `maskRanges`) regression-checked byte-identical. Note: production `shadowList.js` sets `DRAW_FLAG_ONE_PART` on every instanced-group shadow item today (ARCH 2026-10-08 batch 970d9c5: multi-range meshGroups cast only `ranges[0]`), which collapses the per-range mask lookup by design - that is the already-tracked ONE_PART story, not re-litigated here; this test isolates rasterJS.js's masking capability the same way step (a) did. (2) WGSL: `RASTER_INSTANCED_MASK_SHADOW_WGSL = toShadowVertexWgsl(RASTER_INSTANCED_MASK_WGSL)` in `raster.wgsl.js` (only the clip-z line swapped to the sun-shadow convention [0.5,1]; `fs_mask_shadow` reused verbatim, same `maskDiscard`/`texMask`), registered append-only in `index.js` as `rasterShadowInstancedMask`. `raster.wgsl.test.js` additions: layout/z-convention/`fs_mask_shadow` structural checks, regression guards (other shadow variants + the colour `instancedMask` variant unaffected), and 2 mutation tests on the shadow text (cutoff `<`->`<=` widening caught on all 8 zero-texel cells; `fs_mask_shadow`'s discard call deleted is caught structurally, with the unrelated colour-pass `fs_main` discard confirmed untouched). B2 2026-10-09 (S8-B2-16 convex-hull collider option) -> arch-review: `tools/gen-mesh-colliders.mjs --hull` + mesh-json flag `colliderHull: true` (both required - no committed mesh is flagged, so `--check` is 0 diffs by default). Hull = `buildHullProxy` (engine/mesh/colliderProxy.js): 18-DOP support-point sampling (6 face + 12 edge directions of a cube, <= 18 points, deterministic tiebreak) feeds an exact incremental quickhull, so Euler's formula (F=2V-4) pins the output at <= 32 triangles with no post-hoc face decimation; defensive cap-check falls back to the existing prism if ever exceeded. Emitted as plain CCW triangles into the same `collider` array the prism uses - ZERO changes to engine/physics/meshCollide.js or bvh.js, no new convex/GJK primitive (ARCH note honoured). `colliderHull` persists through the binary mesh round-trip in tools/mesh-file.mjs, same as `colliderParts`. Per-mesh build time logged on every run (ms). New test `engine/mesh/hullProxy.test.js` (45 checks): hull <= 32 tris and deterministic on a real rock mesh, every hull vertex is a real mesh vertex (no averaging), outward winding + positive volume, degenerate/flat input falls back to null; a 6-vertex/8-face triangular-prism fixture is reconstructed exactly and swept with `moveCircleMesh` from 8 directions - blocked right at the hull face (never inside), free just outside the footprint; `--hull`/`colliderHull` gating via a temp-dir CLI integration test (default run unaffected, `--hull` switches only the flagged mesh, idempotent on rerun) plus a final `--check` against the real content root (0 diffs). Skill `engine-physics-colliders` updated (1 line + test listed). Per-mesh build time logged on every run (ms). New test `engine/mesh/hullProxy.test.js` (45 checks): hull <= 32 tris and deterministic on a real rock mesh, every hull vertex is a real mesh vertex (no averaging), outward winding + positive volume, degenerate/flat input falls back to null; a 6-vertex/8-face triangular-prism fixture is reconstructed exactly and swept with `moveCircleMesh` from 8 directions - blocked right at the hull face (never inside), free just outside the footprint; `--hull`/`colliderHull` gating via a temp-dir CLI integration test (default run unaffected, `--hull` switches only the flagged mesh, idempotent on rerun) plus a final `--check` against the real content root (0 diffs). Skill `engine-physics-colliders` updated (1 line + test listed). Gates: `node tools/run-tests.mjs --filter hullProxy/meshProxy/meshCollide/colliders/mesh` all PASS (voxelMesh.test.js flaked once under load, unrelated file, PASS alone), `node tools/check-deps.mjs` OK, `node tools/gen-mesh-colliders.mjs --check` 0 would-change. ### S8-B2-10a (kestrel-4) -> ARCH OK (batch 2026-10-13)
`HzbU.srcPitch` appended (block 16 -> 32 B; 0 = srcW; twin `hzbTexel/hzbDownsample(..., pitch)`). New `engine/mesh/occlusion.js` (projectAabbRect, hzbMipPick, nearDepth, aabbOccluded, instanceOccluded, occlusionPass; scratch, 0 alloc) + `occlusion.test.js` (wall culled / peeking kept / +Inf / hzbOn 0 / sway pad, 4800-instance superset vs brute force, fast turn + disocclusion (phase1 U phase2 superset, phase 2 recovers 68/101), invalid HZB, alloc). Test found a real edge bug (rect rows beyond a 1-row top level -> empty loop = "occluded"): kernel 10b must clamp BOTH rect ends to the level (`min(x0>>L, w-1)`).
Choices: ambiguity -> conservative: rect off-screen or any corner w <= 1e-6 -> visible; viewport mapping sx=(x/w*.5+.5)*W, sy=(.5-y/w*.5)*H; fwd/eye/margin in `frame`. Tests: hzb/occlusion/wgsl suites PASS.

### S8-B2-10b (kestrel-4) -> ARCH OK (batch 2026-10-13)
`wgsl/cull.wgsl.js`: CullU +28 words (44 vp[4] | 60 fwd (w = margin) | 64 hzbOn | 65 hzbW | 66 hzbH | 67 hzbLevels | 68 hzbPitch | 69 phase | 70 slot2 | 71 slot3 = 72 words/288 B; note said 76, 72 is the exact packing), `CULL_BUFFERS` 7 (5 hzb f32 read, 6 occl u32 rw). Kernel: `occProject/occNear/occTest/instOccluded` (twin occlusion.js, both rect ends clamped), `emit(o,lod,bf,s0,s1)` shared by phase 1 (slot0/1) and phase 2 (slot2/3, stored lod `occl>>1&1`, band recomputed, never lodPrev). hzbOn 0 = old path, no hzb/occl access (Proxy-verified) and byte-identical to the pre-10b runCull transcription; phase 2 w/ hzbOn 0 emits nothing. Shadows untouched.
Tests (cull.wgsl.test.js, cs_main itself executed in JS): CullU/bindings asserts; instOccluded == twin on 6 HZB shapes (padded pitch, 1-row, 1x1, swayPad); all-sky HZB == hzbOn 0 (dither on); two-phase sets (pending bits, phase 1 rows, phase 2 rows per LOD/range, union = baseline minus occluded, lodPrev untouched). Mutation (unclamped rect end) fails. cull/occlusion/hzb/wgsl suites + passCull PASS, check-deps OK.
NEEDS B1 (10c): (1) pipeline now declares 7 buffers: bind hzb (f32, `hzbPitch` rows for level 0 then dense levels) at 5 and `occl` (4 B x maxInstances) at 6, or a 16 B dummy each when off (passCull.js still binds 5 -> must be updated together). (2) CullU words above: vp = viewProj columns, fwd = unit view forward + w 0.05, hzbOn/W/H/Levels/Pitch (pitch = padded copy row words, 0 = W), phase 0/1 main, 2 re-test; hzbOn 0 on first frame/cut/teleport/resize. (3) Per batch when on: dst2/dst3 (64 B x maxInstances) + args slots slot2/slot3 (rangeCount0/1 records each, written like slot0/1). (4) Phase 2 dispatch = same thread count, same bind group EXCEPT bindings 2/3 = dst2/dst3, uniform phase 2 + fresh HZB at 5; run after copy depth -> hzb build (passHzb) and before raster B (load, no clear); `?occl=` default OFF; invalidate on cut.

- CRASH-PASS-01 (99bab8b9): ARCH CHANGES. (1) `abortPass()` sets `tracePasses = true` permanently. After one aborted pass, every `beginPass` allocates `new Error().stack` for the rest of the session (several passes per frame, which breaks the 0-alloc hot path). Turn it back off once an attributed owner has been logged, or cap it (e.g. at the next 2 aborts / 120 frames). (2) After 3 logs, the glow failure goes silent. Log every 600 like FIX-RING, or expose `_glowFails` in `stats`, so a permanently failing glow is visible.

### OB-054 ground flicker: patch colour world-anchored (kestrel-3, Node only, superseded by OB-073 (batch 2026-10-13))
- Cause: `hA` (tier pick = patch colour) and the close-band jitter were hashed on `cellSz = pow2ceil(t*k)`, so a world point re-rolled colour whenever its distance crossed a step; plus the hard +-0.08 jitter switch at closeBand.
- Fix (both twins, `terrainShade.js` + `shade.wgsl.js`): `PATCH_CELL = 1 m` fixed world cell keys hA and the jitter; jitter amplitude fades smoothstep to 0 over closeBand/2..closeBand. Glyph (hB), glint, features still use `cellSz` (glyph flips remain: 38.50 b/c).
- Test: `terrainShade.test.js` patch colour identical 2..40 m (old code: 1161/1800 changed), close-band continuity; SHADE_WGSL length pin 40434 -> 40766. Suites terrain/shade/wgsl/check-deps green.
- gpucompare: expect terrain rows to change (colour/brightness of every terrain cell, near ground most). 38.50 OB-054a walk metric NOT written (not asked beyond spec; left).
### Arch batch review (PC-B architect opus, 2026-10-11, D-066) - OB-051a/b, OB-052, OB-053a
- **OB-051a/b (cdc6434d + 249929d4 export): ARCH OK -> po-review.** Matches 38.48: bit 7 only set by UiLayer.setCellScaled (scene fg.a is glyphIdx < 95, WGSL gates on `layer == 1`), quadrant from cell parity in WGSL + Canvas2D fast path + uiScale twin, rare-merge degrade as specced, 0 alloc, crosshair scale 1 unchanged, non-UI RT falls back to 1x. Owner check: prompt look at `?pose` + `?force2d=1` once.
- **OB-052 (08cce6b7): ARCH OK -> po-review.** Score angle + 15*dist, keep bonus 5, held-target gate +15 deg / +0.3 m keyed on the incoming `out.targetKey` (rec.key), still 0 alloc, LOS unchanged; tests cover acquire/hold/drop/steal. Note: hysteresis state lives in the caller's `out` - callers must keep reusing one `out` per eye (they do: world.interaction). The vertical-extent follow-up is separate.
- **OB-053a (16e0c101): ARCH OK -> main session gpucompare + 10-nudge GI probe at ob053Heap.** Programmer's precision correction is right (per tier 2.4 cm at 50 m, tier 2 double) and is ACCEPTED, no code change: the tie noise is constant in [0,1] depth (probe: max |dA-dB| = 2^-23 over 500 poses vs DEPTH_TIER 2^-21 = 4x margin), so a metre/slope-scaled bias that shrinks with distance would fall under the noise where the flicker lives; in cells the bleed is only ~1/8 cell at 50 m. Keep 2^-21 (2^-22 leaves < 2x margin with depth24 quantization). 38.49 sentence corrected + "Precision" line added (architecture.md, uncommitted). ### Arch batch review (PC-B architect opus, 2026-10-11, D-066) - OB-061, OB-066, OB-067, OB-063/OB-052b cleanup
- **OB-061 (7ecd6456): ARCH OK -> owner look.** The JS twin and the CPU WebGPU path both handle it: passRaster.js:527 picks instanceOpenPipe on NO_CULL, and the shader flips N on !front_facing. Open question: the WG-4 cull kernel never builds LOD1 entries for mesh groups, because instances.js:489 calls `gpu.accept(g, draw, null)` (m1 = null). So with gpuCull on, trees draw LOD0 only (masked, through instanceMaskPipe 'none') and the dark-LOD1 case cannot happen there. No change needed now. Rule for later: if mesh-group LOD1 ever moves into the kernel, `_cullDraw` must bind `instanceOpenPipe` / `_aoPipe('instanceOpen')` for `lod === 1 && meshIsMasked(g.mesh) && !meshIsMasked(m1)`.
- **OB-066 (2a0f8209): ARCH OK -> po-review.** The slab test does not allocate. LOS runs to tn - 0.05. Hysteresis is pad +0.1 and reach +0.3. Nearest hit wins. Default boxes are read live (loot recs moved on death still work). NPC talk (z+1), pickups (z+0.3), doors (level z 1) and relay (z+1) all get a usable 0.8 x 0.8 x 1.1 m padded box. propPickBox allocates, but only at add time. Non-blocking: (a) an eye inside a padded box targets it in any look direction (tested on purpose; equals the old near-widening); (b) the gondola assertion `d > 2.0 || ...` passes vacuously when d > 2, so pin the pose so it really tests; (c) a moved prop's `pick` goes stale (documented as static).
- **OB-067 (4439bc57): ARCH CHANGES.** (1) terrainShade.js:120-122: `hk`/`cellSz` (and its per-pixel `perCellHashSize` Math.pow/log2 when hashCell < 0) are now dead. Delete them, delete `perCellHashSize` if only tests use it, and note in the ctx/uniform docs that `hashCell` and `handover` no longer affect shadeTerrain (keep the uniform layout). (2) Parity: the WGSL side is tie-safe: dividing by 1.0 and 4.0 is exact, floor/i32 are exact, and the f32 rounding of the calm/closeG threshold is about 1e-6 m. The remaining world_m1 waystoneDown risk is noise in the GPU's own u,v input, which the shade cannot fix. Main session: run gpucompare. If waystoneDown still FAILS, do not widen thresholds (D-039). Make the JS twin derive u,v with the same f32 steps the GPU uses (Math.fround at each step of the cast/reconstruct), as a follow-up step.
- **OB-063 / OB-052b: clean.** No cone/ellipse/near-widening identifiers are left in engine/game/tools. The only remaining copy is the stale desktop build in tools/desktop/dist, which is rebuilt on the next package.

### Arch batch review (PC-B architect opus, 2026-10-11, D-066) - OB-069, OB-068/072, OB-076, OB-077, OB-074/075, INT-HL-01
- **OB-069 (74eb7bb8): ARCH OK.** Head 2 is inside GAME_SAFE_RES and the cap holds (knight 3881 < 4096). Saved recipes without `res` now render the fine head: deterministic, and the look change is intended. randomRecipe already copies kit.defaults.res. Follow-up (non-blocking): `effectiveRes(recipe)` in recipe.js does not know the kit, so tools/chargen ui.js (applyRes, quadLabel, the res selector) and core.js savePackage still read a res-less recipe as 1/1 while compose uses 1/2. Add `effectiveRes(recipe, kit)` and make compose use it, so there is one source.
- **OB-068/072 (e64ce1ab, a6369f82): ARCH OK.** runGame runs once per page (a reload is location.reload), so the document listeners are added once and nothing leaks. The swallow is gated by `pauseResume.gesture()`, which is false unless `active`, and Esc is excluded. Keyboard Lock is feature-detected and released (plus reset) on fullscreen exit. The retry timer is cleared by tick/reset. Minor: pointerdown and click both call lock() on the same gesture. The second request is caught, so this is harmless.
- **OB-076 (0a629f09): ARCH CHANGES.** (1) The bug came from three caps that had drifted apart (targetables MAX_TARGETABLES, sword MAX_TARGETS, targeting MAX_TARGETS_HARD_CAP + DEFAULTS.maxTargets). - **OB-077 (7c2ec031): ARCH OK.** Integer step counters, no rng/trig/clock. aggroNear is O(beasts) with an early exit and no allocation. hpSince/hpAcc are hashed. They are not saved, so a load restarts the 8 s delay (fine).
- **OB-074/075 (0a629f09): ARCH OK.** The rng draw order is unchanged (orbHit and orbKind are still drawn). Corpse state is runtime-only (boars respawn on load), so save compatibility is unaffected. Non-blocking: loot.hashInto hashes neither orbHeld nor gearHeld. Add both (as a small int code) next time loot.js is touched. The toast string is built only when the event fires, which is fine.
- **INT-HL-01 (1d5f9af8): ARCH OK.** Allocation-free: the scratch, terms, closures and colour table are all built at boot. Rays per frame are not hard-capped at 6. LOS runs for each on-screen interactable within 3 m that the cap-reject does not cut, which in practice is a handful. Acceptable. If a dense spot (market, camp) shows up in the profile, add a per-frame LOS budget of 8.

- OB-111 (kestrel-4, ob111): water "disappears in a circle when close" - cause found in Node (JS twin, real world_m1 frame, scratch probe): NOT the under state (updateUnderState stays off at the ford and bank, results identical with fb.under set), not fog, not a near cut (no near cut exists in the water pass). It is the water composite alpha `a = (sceneDepth - waterDepth) / opaqueAt` (waterComposite.js / .wgsl.js): the view-ray path through water is short near the feet (steep angle) and the braid column is only 0.14-0.46 m (OB-107 ford, braid look opaqueAt 1.2, seeThrough 0.45 -> opaque only at path >= 0.54 m), so cells within ~4-6 m of a player standing in/at the shallows stay see-through (floor glyph, tinted) = a hole around the camera; share of opaque water cells by horizontal distance, eye in the ford: 0-4 m 0 %, 4-6 m 27 %, 6-8 m 50 %, >= 10 m ~100 %. Fix is a look decision (e.g. path floor: alpha from max(ray path, column / 0.25), or per-region opaqueAt/seeThrough in design/water-looks.js braid), both twins + gpucompare - awaiting lead. tools/verify-ob111.mjs <port> takes the two stills (not run).
- OB-111 fix (owner: "always show the surface"): composite alpha now uses a path floor, `a = clamp(max(raw - dW, column * 4) / opaqueAt)` for non-sheet, non-sky cells (== column / 0.25 m; waterComposite.js WATER_PATH_FLOOR_K + waterComposite.wgsl.js literal 4.0, JS == GPU, 0 alloc). Shore cells (column < ~0.1 m) still fade; far/grazing/deep water unchanged (ray path already larger). New tools/ob111-water-near.test.mjs (30 frames of the real world_m1; water cells within 5 m with column >= 0.2 m are opaque in fb.waterMask): before 22/547, 4404/9600, 1238/1579, after 543/547, 9600/9600, 1573/1579. waterComposite.test fixture formula updated (0.4 m pool is now opaque everywhere, see-through count assert dropped). gpucompare rows to expect a change: every row with water in the composite (water/pool, ford/braid river rows, waterfall-adjacent, underwaterLake surface cells); run with D-039. Note: the earlier "% opaque by distance" figures undercounted (sky-pass cells have no mask); the hole is real (see before numbers).

- map-loop: PASS, swaps 58, band:late 0, sync bakes 0, max swap 2.31 ms, cache 31.6 MB (= same as pre-dressing: the 32 MB LRU cap bounds it, so the peak is cap-limited). Dressing adds only +0.03..0.27 MB per band bundle (probe MB vs --base, ~32 B/placement, colliders not duplicated), so no byte reduction done; a 1.5 MB margin would need `bandCache.maxBytes`/CACHE_MB policy change (ARCH decision).
- Tests: scatter,geo,detail,band,map-loop 17/18; geo-perf 11/11. FAIL `bandAhead.test.js` "bake-active call max <= 2.5 ms" (5-10 ms) is pre-existing/dressing-related, NOT the trunk check (same 5-6 ms with the check disabled). ASK ARCHITECT: which row/phase spikes (suspect biomeOnly layer rows or terrain row), then split it.

- WM-04: createBeastSim `opts.tileNav` (+ optional `opts.spawn`) turns tileMode on + dormancy at creation (main.js passes `tileNav: !!gridNav`); beastTile test "dormancy before any swap". `_astarCache` is a WeakMap keyed by the grid: freed once BandCache evicts the bundle (no other holder after rebindNav), so no manual eviction.

Fixed-area unchanged. Test villagerTile.test.js (9 checks); `--filter life,outwall,raid,beast` 19/19 PASS.
